### 2. UNIQUE CODE Suffix Algorithm (2715 rows)

All UNIQUE CODEs were rebuilt from component columns using the correct suffix algorithm:

  • Format: P.XXX_N.YY_PDC_CHANNELletter (e.g., _1a, _2b)
  • Suffix letter assigned per Polling District Code based on first-appearance order of Polling Centres
  • Same Polling Centre → same letter across all channels
  • Single-centre districts get no suffix
